## Consistency analysis · change of 2026-11-18, time field without a time (phase 4)

Phase 4 pass for the medium change on branch `fix/match-time-empty-state` (RF-083, E-032, E-033, US2 criterion 6, keys `match.noTime` and `match.addTime`, D51, D52; tasks T089–T092). Read: the uncommitted `spec.md`, `plan.md` ("Change of 2026-11-18", decision 3, date-time-field component, Deviations row), `tasks.md` (T048, T072, T073, T089–T092), ADR-004, `scripts/check-traceability.mjs`, and the current `src/ui/date-time-field.tsx`, `date-time-field-android.tsx`, `date-time-field-shared.ts`, `src/ui/match-form.tsx`, `src/state/new-match-draft.ts`. Nothing was run (no shell, no `git diff`): the old tests were checked by listing the files that mention the time field, not by reading every test. Findings are numbered T089-A-NN.

### Findings

| #         | Severity  | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Where                                                        |
| --------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| T089-A-01 | Important | The RF-083 criterion and E-033 are written for the iOS shape and cannot be satisfied literally on Android: "while it has one, the form shows the time picker and «Quitar hora»" and E-033 "After «Añadir hora»: «12:00», time picker and «Quitar hora»". On Android the picker is a dialog that is closed once the coach confirms or cancels; what stays on screen is the «Hora» button with the time (T090 and the plan say so). Also, with the Android dialog open right after «Añadir hora», «Quitar hora» is not reachable until it is closed, so the E-033 steps need a «Cancelar» in between on Android. Fix the wording in the spec (for example "the time picker, which on Android is the «Hora» button that opens the dialog") and add the «Cancelar» step to T091's Android case, so the tests and the criterion say the same thing. | `spec.md` RF-083 criterion, E-033; `tasks.md` T090, T091     |
| T089-A-02 | Minor     | The spec header has no line for this change's approval (`Approved by` and `D2 approved by` cover earlier approvals), while the plan has "Change of 2026-11-18 … pending". Phase 4 says the approval is recorded in `spec.md` and `plan.md`: add "Change of 2026-11-18 approved by Fernando, <date>" to both when he approves.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `spec.md:3-7`; `plan.md:6`                                   |
| T089-A-03 | Minor     | T090 replaces the test "opening «Hora» and dismissing it keeps no time…" with «Añadir hora» then «Cancelar» keeps 12:00. That is a correct replacement (the old assertion was the behaviour that D52 changes), but the old test also proved a second thing: a dialog that appears saves nothing by itself (the mount guard of `PickerDialog`, from the 2026-11-14 deviation). T090 says the guard stays, but no listed test checks it any more. Keep or add one test where the dialog reports on mount and the time is not changed (after «Añadir hora» it stays 12:00 and after a real confirmation it changes), so the guard keeps a test.                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `tasks.md` T090 "Existing tests updated here"                |
| T089-A-04 | Minor     | Traceability will not fail now (the script only requires IDs of tasks ticked `[x]`, and T089–T092, T072 and T073 are not). When T089 is ticked, its line adds E-033 to the required set: make sure at least one test title of `new-match.time-states.ios.test.tsx` carries E-033 (and the Android file for T090), so the tick of T089 does not depend on T091. Titles already carry E-032 (`src/domain/match-date.test.ts`). The notes added to T048 and T073 sit in lines that do not start with `- [x]`, so they add nothing to the required set.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `scripts/check-traceability.mjs:27-32`; `tasks.md` T089–T091 |
| T089-A-05 | Minor     | `tests/support/new-match-fixtures.ts` and `tests/support/new-match-form.ts` mention `setTime`/`clearTime`/«Quitar hora» and the Android/iOS doubles in `tests/support/` find the «Hora» button; T089 names `new-match-form.ts` and T090 the doubles "only if needed", but `new-match-fixtures.ts` is only a comment. No action beyond checking at T089/T090 that no other test calls the time pickers (the search found only the files the tasks name).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `tasks.md` T089, T090                                        |

### Checks with no finding

- **Spec ↔ plan ↔ tasks.** RF-083, E-032, E-033, US2 criterion 6, the two keys, D51 and D52, the changelog row and the source coverage row agree with the plan's explanation, design and Deviations row and with T089–T092. D51 (12:00 always, also after «Quitar hora») and D52 (Android: «Añadir hora» sets 12:00 and opens the dialog, «Cancelar» keeps 12:00) are in the RF-083 text and are in "Done when" of T089 and T090. No `[DOUBT]` is open. ADR-004's counter (`RF-088`, `E-033`, next `RF-089` and `E-034`) is right.
- **Every criterion has a task.** Without a time (T089, T090): «Sin hora», «Añadir hora», no picker and no «Quitar hora». «Añadir hora» (12:00 in the data, picker and «Quitar hora» shown, no «Sin hora» or «Añadir hora»): T089, T090. «Quitar hora» back to the opening state: T089, T090, T091. A new match starts without a time and is saved without one: T091. 12:00 after a second «Añadir hora» (D51): T089, T090. Saved only with «Guardar» (RF-082): T091 (Back saves nothing). Details half of E-032 and the form that opens with a saved time or without it: T072 and T073. Lists unchanged (RF-074): not touched.
- **The plan's explanation matches the code.** The iPhone symptom is real in `date-time-field.tsx`: the time picker is always mounted with `toLocalDate(date, time)`, whose fallback is `hour = 12`, and «Quitar hora» only calls `onClearTime`. `match-form.tsx` already passes `state.setTime` and `state.clearTime`, and the draft's `setTime` and `clearTime` already do what is needed, so "not touched" is accurate for `match-form.tsx` and `src/state/`. `PickerDialog`, its `useRef` guard and `useState<Mode | null>` are as described. `shownTime(time)` returning `null` without a time is used by both fields today. The Android «Hora» button without a time, which the plan removes, exists today. The only claim not visible in the code is that noon protects against daylight-saving changes; it is a reason, not a fact the code states, and harmless.
- **Layers.** 12:00 is `ADDED_TIME` in `src/ui/date-time-field-shared.ts`, a picker value that reaches the draft through the existing `setTime`; the interface decides nothing about a rule, and `src/domain/`, `src/data/` and `src/state/` are not touched (so no Stryker run). Not splitting 12:00 between the draft and the details screen is a sound choice; the discarded alternatives are reasonable. `NoTime` is a presentational component with no logic. No new dependency and no ADR. The constitution check in the plan is consistent with `docs/constitution.md`.
- **The Android reading.** «Añadir hora» calls `onTimeChange(ADDED_TIME)` and `setOpen("time")`; the dialog opens on `toLocalDate(date, time)` = 12:00 because both updates are batched; «Cancelar» or a dismissal only calls `onClose`, so 12:00 stays. This is what RF-083 and D52 say, and it keeps the deviation of 2026-11-14 intact (12:00 is stored because the coach pressed «Añadir hora», not because a picker appeared). Only the wording problem in T089-A-01 remains.
- **The split of E-032.** Putting the details half in T072 and T073 (D3) is acceptable: the details screen does not exist, so a test of it cannot be written now, and T091 covers the form half with the real «Guardar». E-032 already has a domain test with its ID, and T072's "Done when" now carries the criterion, so the half is not lost. Traceability does not fail now or at the tick of T089–T092 (see T089-A-04). Open point for D3: T072 must not be ticked without a test titled E-032 for the details.
- **The old tests to update.** For each file the tasks list (`new-match.date-time.test.tsx`, `.edge.test.tsx`, `.date-time-native.test.tsx`, `.date-time-native.ios.test.tsx`, `.date-time-locale.test.tsx`, `tests/e2e/us2-flow.test.ts` and the Maestro flow), the tests that are replaced are those whose assertion was the old behaviour (a time picker or a «Hora» button with no time, «Cancelar» leaving no time, a second «Quitar hora»), and each replacement keeps its intent; the others only press «Añadir hora» first and keep their assertions. Coverage is not weakened, with the one reservation in T089-A-03.
- **Level and Security.** Medium is right: it changes existing behaviour (RF-083, E-032, US2) and touches no `src/domain/`, so it is not small, and it adds no feature, so it is not large. Security is not needed: no dependency, data, navigation or configuration is touched (the texts in `es.json` are not configuration), as T092 says.

### Verdict

**Ready for approval, once the Important finding T089-A-01 is fixed in the spec and T091** (one sentence in RF-083 and E-033, and a «Cancelar» step in the Android case of T091). There is no blocking finding and no open `[DOUBT]`. The minor findings (T089-A-02 to T089-A-05) can be done with the approval or during phase 6. No code is touched before Fernando approves the spec and the plan; the approval line is to be recorded in both (T089-A-02).

## Codex review · fix/match-time-empty-state (2026-11-18)

Run: `scripts/review.sh specs/001-planificador-v1` at commit `de83c6d` (branch `fix/match-time-empty-state` against `main`), 2026-11-18 09:07. Full output: [codex-review.md](codex-review.md).

| #    | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | File                                  | Severity (Codex) | Status                                                                      |
| ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ---------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| F-01 | **Keyboard avoidance omits the form's screen offset.** `keyboardVerticalOffset` defaults to zero, so React Native underestimates keyboard overlap: lower controls, such as «Guardar», can stay partly hidden at maximum scroll in short landscape views. Current tests check component structure, not geometry. **Concrete fix:** supply the real top offset (headers and modal positioning), add a keyboard-geometry regression, and verify «Guardar» on an iPhone in landscape. Native verification remains pending. | `src/ui/form-scroll.tsx:23`           | IMPORTANT        | Fixed on `fix/form-keyboard-offset` (2026-11-20); see the last section      |
| F-02 | **Daylight-saving normalisation can make the picker disagree with the saved time.** Under `Europe/Madrid`, choosing 02:30 and then changing the date to 29 March 2026 feeds 03:30 to the picker, while the draft, the displayed text and the saved value keep 02:30. Codex verified this directly. **Concrete fix:** use an independent reference date for the time-only picker and add a DST regression that covers date changes.                                                                                     | `src/ui/date-time-field-shared.ts:26` | MINOR            | Fixed on `fix/picker-dst-drift` (2026-11-21); see the last section          |
| F-03 | **Shared-store updates still run during render (existing M-01).** The `useState` initialiser of the team edit screen calls `openTeam()`, which publishes Zustand updates while rendering and triggers React's cross-component update warning. The new-player route and `useOpenTeam()` repeat the pattern. **Concrete fix:** initialise through the existing focus effects, guard rendering by team ownership, and assert that navigation emits no React warnings.                                                     | `src/app/teams/[teamId]/edit.tsx:19`  | MINOR            | Fixed on `fix/team-forms-render-updates` (2026-11-21); see the last section |

Codex's validation: types, lint, source formatting, traceability, focused or skipped-test detection and Gitleaks passed. With Jest cache writes suppressed in memory, **2,930 tests passed**; **233 tests across 16 suites were blocked by temporary-file permissions**. Codex did not confirm full verification, coverage, mutation testing, fresh dependency scans or native behaviour. Maestro, D3 and the documented device checks remain pending, so this review does not establish full-spec completion.

## Maestro run on Android · fix/maestro-android-flows (2026-11-21)

First run of the Maestro flows on Android. Form `small-change-maestro-android-flows.md` (approved by Fernando, 2026-11-20). Flows at commit `c4e983f`.

- **Tools:** Maestro 2.12.1; Android emulator `Medium_Phone_API_37.0` (Android 17, arm64, 1080x2400, device language English). The data partition of that emulator was reduced from 10 G to 4 G to fit the free disk (backup `config.ini.bak` in its folder). For iOS: the iPhone 17 of the earlier run.
- **Build:** Release APK, `expo prebuild` and `./gradlew assembleRelease -PreactNativeArchitectures=arm64-v8a` on `origin/main` at `bcbcd43` (the merge of PR 10), installed on the emulator with the animations off.

| Flow                    | Android phone emulator                                    | iPhone 17 simulator                   |
| ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `us1-teams-and-players` | passes                                                    | passes                                |
| `us1-form-interruption` | passes (flow unchanged since the first run)               | passes (run of 2026-11-20, unchanged) |
| `us2-match-and-call-up` | stops at the score field (F-A1); date and time steps work | passes                                |
| `us3-planning`          | stops after removing the last overtime (F-A2)             | not run again (flow unchanged)        |
| `us3-interruption`      | passes                                                    | passes                                |
| `us4-warnings`          | not run: tablet flow, no Android tablet emulator          | not run again (flow unchanged)        |

On the first Android run only `us1-form-interruption` passed. The flow problems were fixed in this branch (see `summary.md`).

**Verification:** `npm run verify` on the flows commit: 251 suites, 3169 tests, "All checks passed". Mutation testing did not run: nothing in `src/domain/` changed.

**Findings about the app, outside this change** (their own branches):

- **F-A1 · IMPORTANT · Android keyboard covers the focused field.** Match form: after `scrollUntilVisible` of «Notas», a tap on «Notas» or on a score field opens the keyboard and the form does not scroll: the field and what is typed are under the keyboard (screenshot checked; typed «hola notas» in «Notas» and it is not visible). This is the problem of F-01 (fixed in PR 10 only for iOS: `automaticallyAdjustKeyboardInsets` is iOS only and Android keeps `behavior="height"`). RF-076. **Fixed on `fix/android-keyboard-offset` (2026-11-21); see the last section.**
- **F-A2 · IMPORTANT · Android grid stuck after removing the last overtime.** A mark in PR1 scrolls the grid sideways; removing the overtime leaves it scrolled, so P1 and P2 are behind the name column and a swipe does not bring them back. The cells are in the accessibility tree but off screen. RF-042, RF-057. **Fixed on `fix/android-grid-scroll` (2026-11-21); see the last section.**
- **F-A3 · MINOR · Android link lost right after the launch.** `launchApp` then `openLink` of `planificadorperiodos://matches/99` ends on the list of teams, with no «No se ha encontrado». The same link as the launch intent (`am start` on a stopped app) or to a running app works. **Accepted as a limit on 2026-11-21 (decision of Fernando, chat), not fixed:** measured on the Android 17 emulator with the same link after `monkey` launched the app, the link is lost when it arrives 0.3 s after the launch and handled when it arrives 0.8, 1.5, 2.5 or 4 s after. While the database opens, `RootLayout` returns nothing (`src/app/_layout.tsx`), so no navigator is mounted and the navigation of a link that arrives in that fraction of a second has nowhere to go; a link that is the launch itself, or one sent to a running app, works. A coach would have to tap a link in under a second from opening the app. Fixing it would mean keeping the links that arrive while the database opens and replaying them afterwards, a change of navigation with the risk of navigating twice when the link is the launch itself; it is not worth it for this window. The Maestro flow `us3-interruption` waits for the list of teams before each link for that reason.
- Also seen: on a phone the warning strip is bounded and shows only its first banner (known, RF-080), which is why `us4-warnings` needs a tablet.

**What this run does NOT cover:** `us4-warnings` on Android, Android in landscape, a real Android device, other Android versions, a device in Spanish (the date dialog showed English names), and the iPad. `us3-planning` and `us2-match-and-call-up` cannot finish on Android until F-A2 and F-A1 are fixed.

**Tasks:** T036 is ticked (both of its flows pass on iOS and Android). T048, T056 and T060 stay unticked.

## Verification and Codex review · fix/android-keyboard-offset, F-A1 (2026-11-21)

Form: `small-change-form-android-keyboard.md` (approved by Fernando, 2026-11-21). Fix at commit `937b497`: `src/ui/form-scroll.tsx` and `tests/app/form-scroll.keyboard.test.tsx`. This fixes F-A1 of the Maestro run on Android (section above) and the Android half of F-01.

**Verification:** `npm run verify` on `937b497`: 251 suites, 3176 tests, "20/20 checks passed", "All checks passed", exit code 0. A first run had one failed check, the React Native Directory check of Expo Doctor ("unexpected server response", a network answer); `npx expo-doctor` passed straight after and so did the full run again. Mutation testing did not run: nothing in `src/domain/` changed.

**Tests:** six new tests in `tests/app/form-scroll.keyboard.test.tsx`. Three failed before the change for the right reasons (no scroll when the focused field is under the keyboard, no scroll with an existing offset, the keyboard listener not removed on unmount) and pass after it; the other three (a field already above the keyboard, no field focused, iOS) pass before and after, to keep those cases as they were. They give the form a keyboard event and a focused field at a chosen height: they check the scroll it asks for, not a real keyboard.

**Emulator (the real proof):** Android 17 emulator `Medium_Phone_API_37.0`, 1080x2400, Release build (arm64) of the fix, Gboard.

- Portrait, new-match form: scrolling to «Notas» and tapping it opens the keyboard and the form scrolls so that the field and its text («hola notas») sit above it. Before the change the form did not move and the field was under the keyboard (reproduced on 2026-11-21).
- `us2-match-and-call-up` on Android now goes past the score fields: it taps «Local», «Visitante» and both score fields (number keyboard), moves from one to another with the keyboard open, scrolls down to «Guardar» and taps it (the message «Escribe los dos resultados o ninguno.» shows), and goes on until it types the notes. It stops there, at the 500-character notes (see below).
- Landscape: «Notas» and what is typed are visible, in the keyboard's own full-screen editor (Android shows it for a text field in landscape; the form is not visible behind it).

**What this does NOT cover:** reaching «Guardar» in landscape; the Android tablet; a real Android device; other Android versions; a keyboard other than Gboard; the team and player forms (they share the component and were not opened).

**Found along the way (not part of this change):** `us2-match-and-call-up` on Android now stops when it types the 500-character notes: `inputText` took about 0.6 s per character on the emulator and Maestro gave up after 120 s (`DEADLINE_EXCEEDED`, "Device server died during inputText"). It is a limit of the flow on Android, not of the app. The flow needs the text in chunks or another way to fill the field. It stays open for its own branch.

**Codex run:** `scripts/review.sh specs/001-planificador-v1 origin/main` on `937b497`. Full output: [codex-review.md](codex-review.md), which replaces the previous one.

**Verdict as stated by Codex:** 0 BLOCKING, 1 IMPORTANT, 0 MINOR. No blocking finding, so the phase 8 gate is met.

Form: `small-change-form-android-grid-scroll.md` (approved by Fernando, 2026-11-21). Fix at commit `2d9888f`: `src/ui/grid/match-grid.tsx` and `src/ui/grid/match-grid.offset.test.tsx`. This fixes F-A2 of the Maestro run on Android.

It does nothing when the offset is in range, which is the case on iOS, but that is reasoning and not a test.

## Maestro `us2` on Android, F-A4 and Codex review · fix/maestro-us2-android-notes (2026-11-21)

Forms: `small-change-maestro-us2-android-notes.md` and `small-change-form-phone-strip-portrait.md` (both approved by Fernando, 2026-11-21). Commits: flow and tests `700e83a`, the strip `3402c24`, and the follow-up of the Codex review of this section.

**What changed in the app (F-A4).** `src/ui/grid/tablet-budget.ts` and `src/ui/warnings/warning-strip.tsx`: the warning strip of a phone in portrait is bounded at 220 pt (`PHONE_PORTRAIT_STRIP_MAX_HEIGHT`) instead of the 60 pt of a landscape phone, so the two banners are in view. Landscape phones (60) and tablets (80) are unchanged. Found when `us2-match-and-call-up` stopped at «Convocatoria fuera del rango» on an Android phone: the strip showed about one line of the first banner.

**The number.** The form was approved with 160 pt. The Codex review (finding 1 below) noted that two banners with their padding and margins do not fit in 160, and measuring on the emulator confirmed it: with 160 the second banner was cut. With 220, both banners are fully in view on the Android 17 emulator at 411 pt wide (two lines each, and the first legend line below) and at 375 pt wide (`wm size 984x2160`, the first banner on three lines, both banners complete, the legend line cut). The form records the change of number.

**Flow changes (T048).** `e2e/us2-match-and-call-up.yaml`: the 501 characters of the notes in three pieces of 167 (Maestro gives each command 120 s; typing took 0.18 to 0.6 s per character); on Android one swipe before the call-up taps; the number-keyboard subflow centres the «Local» label on Android before tapping it (`hideKeyboard` there leaves the form when the keyboard is already closed, which was seen); the flow scrolls to the banner and to the last row of the 13-player grid instead of asserting them where they are. Static tests: `typedRuns` and `typedTexts` in `tests/support/us2-flow.ts` read the notes as the join of consecutive steps, and `tests/e2e/us2-flow.typing.test.ts` requires at least two pieces of at most 170 characters (it fails with one step of 501).

**Tests.** `tests/app/match-screen.phone-portrait.test.tsx`: eight tests for the strip (the bound in portrait, when it scrolls, the grid keeps a header row and two rows on a 360 × 640 phone, landscape and tablet bounds unchanged); five failed before the change.

**Verification:** `npm run verify` on `3402c24`: 3190 tests, "20/20 checks passed", "All checks passed", exit code 0. It was run again after the follow-up (see the commit of this section). Mutation testing did not run: nothing in `src/domain/` changed.

**Maestro results (Android 17 phone emulator, Release build of the final code; iPhone 17 simulator).**

| Flow                    | Android phone emulator                 | iPhone 17 simulator                                  |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `us2-match-and-call-up` | passes, 292 steps                      | passes, 284 steps (app built before the F-A4 change) |
| `us3-planning`          | passes, 208 steps                      | passes, 204 steps (run on 2026-11-21, before F-A4)   |
| `us3-interruption`      | passes, 100 steps (with the 160 build) | passes (run on 2026-11-20)                           |

`us3-interruption` on Android was last run with the 160 pt build, not the 220 pt one; the flow does not read the banners. Two runs of `us3-*` on the first try did not start: Maestro's driver did not come up on a freshly booted emulator ("System UI isn't responding" dialog, dismissed with «Wait»).

**What this does NOT cover:** the F-A4 change itself on iOS (the iPhone simulator has the build from before it); `us4-warnings` on Android (a tablet flow); a real phone; scaled-up system fonts (the banners may need scrolling); the Android tablet; a Spanish-configured device.

**Codex run:** `scripts/review.sh specs/001-planificador-v1 origin/main` on `3402c24`. Full output: [codex-review.md](codex-review.md), which replaces the previous one.

**Verdict as stated by Codex:** 0 BLOCKING, 2 IMPORTANT, 1 MINOR. No blocking finding, so the phase 8 gate is met.

| #   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | **IMPORTANT. The portrait height budget underestimates the banners:** each banner takes 32 pt of margins and 24 pt of padding, so 160 leaves 48 pt for the wrapped texts; the tests inject a 150 pt measurement and do not show that both banners fit. | Fixed in this branch: the bound is 220 pt, with the budget in the comment of the constant (about 175 at 411 pt wide and 215 at 375 pt wide). Checked on the emulator at both widths. Jest has no layout engine, so the tests still read the bound; the emulator check is the proof of the geometry. |
| 2   | **IMPORTANT. Current verification and completion records are missing.**                                                                                                                                                                                | Fixed in this branch: this section, `summary.md` (after explanation) and T048 (ticked).                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 3   | **MINOR. The static test does not protect the notes chunking:** Codex replaced the three pieces by one 501-character step and the 19 US2 tests still passed.                                                                                           | Fixed in this branch: `tests/e2e/us2-flow.typing.test.ts` fails in that case (checked by making the same replacement).                                                                                                                                                                              |

Codex's other category results: no scope, constitution, layer or glossary finding, no test deleted or skipped, no security finding, no simplicity finding. Codex could not run the screen tests in its environment and did not confirm the full verification or the native behaviour.

**Tasks:** T048 is ticked (the flow passes on both platforms). T060 stays unticked.

**Tests:** five new tests in `tests/app/team-forms.render-updates.test.tsx`. Three failed before the change with the real warning «Cannot update a component (`TeamScreen`) while rendering a different component» (the edit form and the new-player form of the same team opened over the team screen, and a second team's screen over the first) and pass after it. The other two (the edit form of another team shows that team's data; an unknown team shows the not-found text) pass before and after. A first version of the hook skipped the fresh read of the team on the first focus of a form, and an existing test caught it («a team deleted after it was edited once shows the not-found message the next time the route opens», `edit-team.form.test.tsx`): the forms now read the team afresh on their first focus and, later, only when the store holds another team.

**Android emulator:** Android 17 emulator `Medium_Phone_API_37.0`, Release build (arm64) of the fix: `us1-teams-and-players` (132 steps) and `us1-form-interruption` (66 steps) pass; they open the team screen, the edit form and the new-player form.

**What this does NOT cover:** iOS (neither a simulator nor a device ran the change), a real Android device, and the empty first frame of each route on a slow device (it lasts until the focus effect runs).

**Codex run:** `scripts/review.sh specs/001-planificador-v1 origin/main` on `723f84c`. Full output: [codex-review.md](codex-review.md), which replaces the previous one.

**Verdict as stated by Codex:** 0 BLOCKING, 1 IMPORTANT, 0 MINOR. No blocking finding, so the phase 8 gate is met.

| #   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | **IMPORTANT. Verification records and the after explanation are missing; `summary.md` still teaches that `openTeam` runs before the first render and lists F-03 as pending; T081 describes the removed initialiser.** | Fixed in this branch: this section; `summary.md` has the after explanation and the corrected teaching paragraph, and F-03 is no longer pending; T081 says the initialiser is gone. The unverified checks (iOS, real device) are listed above. |

Codex's other category results: no scope, constitution, layer or glossary finding, no test deleted, skipped or weakened, no security finding, no simplicity finding. Codex could not run Jest in its environment and did not confirm the full verification or the native behaviour.

## Security and verification · fix/osv-unused-exceptions (2026-11-21)

Form: `small-change-form-osv-unused-exceptions.md` (approved by Fernando, 2026-11-21). Change at commit `bf2c2fe`: two `[[IgnoredVulns]]` entries and two header lines of `osv-scanner.toml`. Nothing else.

**Verification:** `npm run verify` on `bf2c2fe`: "20/20 checks passed", "All checks passed", exit code 0, and the osv-scanner step no longer prints «osv-scanner.toml has unused ignores» (it did on the earlier runs, for exactly these two ids). Tests and mutation testing are not affected: no code changed.

**Security (role run by a subagent, read-only):** no findings, all five checks run.

1. Both advisories are withdrawn (`https://api.osv.dev/v1/vulns/<id>`): `GHSA-gcqx-w48m-w2h4` (deepmerge) on 2026-11-16 and `GHSA-fc5q-39xc-vjpf` (picomatch) on 2026-11-18, both «Withdrawn Advisory».
2. `osv-scanner scan source --lockfile=package-lock.json` scanned 1279 packages and ended with «No issues found», with no unused-ignores warning.
3. The remaining seven exceptions (glob, minimatch, four of `qs`, esbuild) each have a reason, an approval and an expiry of at most 90 days from it (2027-02-08 for the ones approved on 2026-11-10, 90 days; 2027-02-10 for esbuild, approved on 2026-11-13, 89 days), and all seven are still used by the scan.
4. `scripts/check-npm-audit.mjs` has no hard-coded id: it reads the `[[IgnoredVulns]]` entries at run time. Run as `scripts/verify.sh` runs it: «npm audit: no unapproved high or critical advisories», exit 0.
5. Outside `specs/` nothing mentions the two ids; in `specs/` they appear only as history (the expo-sdk58 patches form, `000-puesta-en-marcha/review.md`, earlier sections of this file).

The change only removes exceptions, so it can only make the scan stricter: if an advisory for the same package comes back with a new id, the scan fails until it is looked at.

**Minor note, not part of this change:** `osv-scanner.toml` still uses `ignoreUntil`, which osv-scanner reports as deprecated in favour of `effectiveUntil`. Migrating it is a separate small change, and `scripts/check-npm-audit.mjs` (line 20) reads `ignoreUntil`, so the script would change with it. Recorded as pending.

## Verification and Codex review · fix/picker-dst-drift, F-02 (2026-11-21)

Form: `small-change-form-picker-dst-drift.md` (approved by Fernando, 2026-11-21). Fix at commit `21566a4`: `src/ui/date-time-field-shared.ts` (new `toLocalTime`), `src/ui/date-time-field.tsx`, `src/ui/date-time-field-android.tsx`, and the tests (reworked after the Codex review, see below). This fixes F-02 of the Codex review of 2026-11-18.

**Verification:** `npm run verify` on `21566a4`, and again after the rework of the tests with the host in UTC: "20/20 checks passed", "All checks passed", exit code 0. Mutation testing did not run: nothing in `src/domain/` changed.

**Tests.** The first version set `process.env.TZ` inside the tests; the Codex review showed that this does not change the zone `Date` uses in Jest (the tests passed only because this machine is in Spain) and that the date-change test never changed the date. Fixed: `tests/setup/time-zone.js` is a Jest `globalSetup` that sets `TZ=Europe/Madrid` for the whole suite (checked with the host in UTC: the full `npm run verify` passes with `TZ=UTC`, 3211 tests), `src/ui/date-time-field.dst.test.tsx` drives the field through a parent that keeps the draft (nine tests, iOS and Android), and `src/ui/date-time-field.zones.test.ts` runs the real helpers in five child Node processes started with `TZ` set (Europe/Madrid, America/New_York, Australia/Sydney, Australia/Lord_Howe, UTC), reads back the zone each used and checks that every time from 00:00 to 23:59 comes back unchanged. With the old conversion put back in memory, the six tests about the picker fail (the three of the date picker and of the helper pass, as they should) and with the fix all pass. Five older assertions pinned the match's day inside the time picker's value (`[2026, 11, 11, 12, 0]`); the change removes exactly that, so they now compare only the time of day (`[12, 0]`), as the form said. 992 tests of `src/ui` and `tests/app` passed on the first version, 3211 in the whole suite after the fix.

**Android emulator (the real proof):** Android 17 emulator `Medium_Phone_API_37.0` in the time zone `Europe/Madrid`, Release build (arm64), new-match form: «Fecha» to 29 March 2026, «Añadir hora», the dial on 2 and 30, «Aceptar». Before the change (build of the F-03 branch) the field shows **03:30**: the dialog moved the chosen 02:30 to the next valid time, so the saved value was wrong too, which is worse than the picker only disagreeing with the text. After the change the field shows **02:30** and reopening the dialog shows 02:30.

**What this does NOT cover:** iOS (neither a simulator nor a device ran the change; the inline picker gets the same `toLocalTime`), a real phone, and time zones outside the five of the tests. The reference day 15 January 2001 has no clock change in any zone in use; that is reasoning, not a test of every zone.

**Codex run:** `scripts/review.sh specs/001-planificador-v1 origin/main` on the commit that follows; its output is in [codex-review.md](codex-review.md) and its verdict is recorded below.

**Codex runs of this branch.** First run, on `4f1d199`: 1 BLOCKING (the time-zone tests assigned `process.env.TZ` inside Jest, which does not change the zone `Date` uses, so they only passed in a machine in Spain and failed with the host in UTC) and 1 IMPORTANT (the date-change test never changed the component's date), both fixed in `0c97c20` as described under «Tests». Second run, on `0c97c20`: **no BLOCKING or IMPORTANT finding, one MINOR** (the table of tests in `summary.md` described the first version: eleven tests, eight failing); fixed in this branch. Codex confirmed that the 15 new tests pass and that putting the old conversion back makes six of them fail. Full output of the second run: [codex-review.md](codex-review.md). Codex could not run the whole suite or the CI; both pass in this repository (`npm run verify` with the host in UTC: 3211 tests, "All checks passed").

## Maestro on iOS with the Release build of `main` · docs/ios-verification-2026-11-21 (2026-11-21)

The changes of 2026-11-21 (F-A2 the grid after the last overtime, F-A4 the warning strip of a phone in portrait, F-03 the team screens, F-02 the time picker) had been tested on an Android emulator only, and each pull request listed iOS as not tested. This run closes that gap for what Maestro can see.

- **Build:** `npx expo run:ios --configuration Release --device <iPhone 17> --no-bundler` on `main` at `3fc01a7` (the merge of the pull request of F-02, so it contains F-01 to F-04 and the Android fixes; the only later merge is documentation). The same `.app` was installed on the iPad simulator.
- **Tools:** Maestro 2.12.1; iPhone 17 (`26CB0B49-2377-485C-A49E-798122576A27`) and iPad Pro 11-inch (M5) (`9507BB90-40DB-4B28-987F-5409E82EA72D`, rotated to landscape), both on iOS 27.2.

| Flow                    | Device                      | Result            |
| ----------------------- | --------------------------- | ----------------- |
| `us1-teams-and-players` | iPhone 17, portrait         | passes, 124 steps |
| `us1-form-interruption` | iPhone 17, portrait         | passes, 64 steps  |
| `us2-match-and-call-up` | iPhone 17, portrait         | passes, 284 steps |
| `us3-planning`          | iPhone 17, portrait         | passes, 204 steps |
| `us3-interruption`      | iPhone 17, portrait         | passes, 100 steps |
| `us4-warnings`          | iPad Pro 11-inch, landscape | passes, 357 steps |

What these runs show for each change on iOS: the team screen, the edit-team and new-player forms open and save (F-03, `us1-*`); a match with overtimes and their removal, with the call-up and the grid reachable (F-A2, `us3-planning`); the banners and legend on a phone in portrait and a tablet in landscape (F-A4, `us2-match-and-call-up`, `us4-warnings`); and the time field of the match form (F-02, `us2-match-and-call-up`: on iOS the flow only asserts «12:00» after «Añadir hora»).

**What this does NOT cover:**

- **F-02 on iOS:** the flows never pick a time on a daylight-saving day, and the iOS time wheels are not visible to Maestro (decision of 2026-11-20), so the change of the inline time picker is only shown not to break the form. The 29 March scenario was checked on the Android emulator only.
- The iPad in portrait, an iPhone in landscape, a real iPhone or iPad, the 100 ms repaint measurement of T060 (by hand on the 5th-generation iPad), and Android on a tablet emulator for `us4-warnings`.
- **Disk:** to fit the free space, the iOS build cache of the project (`DerivedData`, 4.4 GB, regenerable) was deleted after the build, and the app was kept in a copy to install it on the iPad.

**Tasks:** T060 stays unticked: its flow still has to pass on an Android tablet and the 100 ms measurement is still to be done by hand.

## Security review at the D2 close (T061) (2026-11-21)

Scope: the whole D2 delivery as it stands on `origin/main` (`b8a57c5`, clean tree, branch `docs/t061-d2-close`), against the audit of 2026-11-13 ("Security · D2 close") and the later ones (T087 and T088, Expo SDK 58 patches, osv-scanner exceptions). Role: `.agents/roles/security.md`; checklist: `docs/security.md`, "Audit of each change". The four areas asked by T061 are: the D2 dependencies, the backup plugin and configuration, the data layer and the navigation. Constitution principles 3 (offline, nothing leaves the device) and 5 (children's data, no logs, no Android backup) are checked in each. Nothing in the code was changed, no dependency was installed, and `scripts/verify.sh`, Stryker and the Jest suite were not run (a full Stryker run was using the CPU).

### What was run

| Check                                                                                                                                                                 | Result                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `npm audit --omit=dev --json` piped to `node scripts/check-npm-audit.mjs` (as `verify.sh` does)                                                                       | "no unapproved high or critical advisories", exit 0. Counts: production tree 20 moderate, 0 high, 0 critical; full tree 36 moderate, 0 high, 0 critical                           |
| `osv-scanner scan source --lockfile=package-lock.json`                                                                                                                | "No issues found"; 9 vulnerabilities filtered by the 7 `osv-scanner.toml` entries, each with reason, approval and an expiry of 2027-02-08 or 2027-02-10; no unused-ignore warning |
| `gitleaks git --redact --no-banner`                                                                                                                                   | 220 commits scanned, no leaks                                                                                                                                                     |
| `semgrep scan --config p/typescript --config p/react --config p/secrets --error` over `src scripts plugins app.config.js`                                             | exit 0, no findings (`verify.sh` scans `src scripts`; `plugins` and `app.config.js` added here)                                                                                   |
| `npx expo prebuild --no-install --clean` in two temporary copies outside the repository (scratchpad), one without `APP_VARIANT` and one with `APP_VARIANT=production` | Both finished; no `ios/` or `android/` was created in the repository (`git status` clean)                                                                                         |
| `xattr -l` on `Documents/SQLite` of the app containers left by the Maestro runs on the iOS simulators                                                                 | iPhone 17 and iPad Pro 11-inch (M5): `com.apple.metadata:com_apple_backup_excludeItem: com.apple.MobileBackup` on the folder that holds `planificador.db`, `-wal` and `-shm`      |

### 1. D2 dependencies

Compared `package.json` and `package-lock.json` with `plan.md`, "New dependencies".

| Package                                 | Plan                  | Installed (lock) | Licence    | Install script | Native code and network use                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------------- | --------------------- | ---------------- | ---------- | -------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `expo-sqlite`                           | `~58.0.17` at T024    | 58.0.18          | MIT        | none           | Native SQLite module; no permission in its Android manifest; no `fetch` or socket in its JS build; plugin has no options   |
| `drizzle-orm`                           | `^0.45.4`             | 0.45.4           | Apache-2.0 | none           | Pure JS; no dependencies; only its `expo-sqlite` entry points are imported from `src/`                                     |
| `drizzle-kit` (dev)                     | `^0.31.11`            | 0.31.11          | MIT        | none           | Build tool; its `esbuild` copies have install scripts (see below); never in the app (`npm ls --omit=dev` has no `esbuild`) |
| `zustand`                               | `^5.1.0`              | 5.1.0            | MIT        | none           | Pure JS, no dependencies, `scripts: {}`                                                                                    |
| `better-sqlite3` (dev) and its `@types` | `^13.1.1`, `^9.6.0`   | 13.1.1, 9.6.0    | MIT        | none           | `devOptional`; imported only from `tests/` (grep: four files, all under `tests/`)                                          |
| `@expo/ui`                              | `~58.0.28` (template) | 58.0.29          | MIT        | none           | Used only for `DateTimePicker` (two files in `src/ui/`). Its Android image loader uses OkHttp, but no image view is used   |
| `expo-splash-screen`                    | template              | 58.0.14          | MIT        | none           | Used in `src/app/_layout.tsx` only (`preventAutoHideAsync`, `hideAsync`)                                                   |
| `react-native-gesture-handler`          | `~3.3.0`, D3 (RF-052) | 3.3.0            | MIT        | none           | Not imported in `src/`; it is a peer of `expo-router`, so it stays. No permission                                          |

- **No package outside the plan was added after D2 started.** The 2026-11-17 patch bumps (`expo`, `expo-router`, `expo-sqlite`, `@expo/ui`, `expo-dev-client`, `expo-web-browser`, `jest-expo`) are recorded in `plan.md` and were audited on 2026-11-17; the lock still has only `https://registry.npmjs.org/` URLs, an integrity hash on every entry, and no new install script.
- **Install scripts in the lock (5, all development or optional):** three `esbuild` copies (`0.18.20` under `@esbuild-kit/core-utils`, `0.25.12`, and `0.28.2` under `tsx`), `fsevents` and `@parcel/watcher`. None is in the app. The `esbuild` 0.18.20 advisory is the approved exception of 2026-11-13.
- **`better-sqlite3` 13.1.1** has no install script and no `node-gyp` step in the lock: its tarball ships prebuilt `.node` binaries (`prebuilds/darwin-arm64.node`, `linux-x64.node`, and others). The plan said "built or downloaded at install". This changes nothing for the app (it is `devOptional`, nothing in `src/` imports it, and it has no `expo-module.config.json` for autolinking), but the binaries are trusted through the lock's integrity hash and not built from source (SEC-T061-6).
- **Licences of the non-development tree:** MIT for the app packages. The few non-permissive ones are build tools and never in the app bundle: `lightningcss` and its platform packages (MPL-2.0, Expo/Metro CSS), `node-forge` (`BSD-3-Clause OR GPL-2.0`, dual-licensed, Expo CLI), `caniuse-lite` (CC-BY-4.0, data), `argparse` (Python-2.0). No GPL-only or AGPL entry. I did not inspect the bundle to prove the tools are absent from it.
- **Maintenance:** every D2 package is current (patch versions of SDK 58 or the latest minor of its line); `drizzle-orm` stays on 0.45, as the plan decided (1.0 is a release candidate). `@types/better-sqlite3` 9.6.0 is older than `better-sqlite3` 13 (known, in the plan).
- **Unused packages:** `expo-web-browser`, `expo-image`, `expo-glass-effect` and `expo-device` are in `package.json` (phase 0 template) and imported by no file in `src/`, `tests/`, `plugins/` or `scripts/`; they are autolinked native modules and are not in the `plan.md` table (SEC-T061-3).

### 2. Backup plugin and configuration

Prebuild output read in both temporary copies (`APP_VARIANT` unset and `production`).

- **`app.json`:** `android.allowBackup: false`; `./plugins/with-backup-exclusion.js` listed once; `android.blockedPermissions` has the four permissions of the checklist; scheme `planificadorperiodos`; no `permissions` list, no `extra`, no `updates` section, no keys; `experiments.typedRoutes: true`. `app.config.js` is 25 lines: it reads only `process.env.APP_VARIANT`, does no network, file or `eval` work, and adds `INTERNET` to the blocked list only when the value is exactly `"production"`.
- **Android manifest, both variants:** `<application>` has `android:allowBackup="false"`, `android:dataExtractionRules="@xml/data_extraction_rules"` and `android:fullBackupContent="@xml/full_backup_content"`, once each. `data_extraction_rules.xml` has 18 `domain=` entries (nine in `<cloud-backup>`, nine in `<device-transfer>`); `full_backup_content.xml` has 9.
- **Android permissions:** without `APP_VARIANT`: `INTERNET` present (needed by Metro in development); `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`, `VIBRATE` and `WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` carry `tools:node="remove"`. With `APP_VARIANT=production`: the same four plus `INTERNET` carry `tools:node="remove"`. The debug overlay manifest (`android/app/src/debug/AndroidManifest.xml`) adds `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`, which only exists in the debug variant. T088 holds; SEC-D2-1 is closed for the permissions in the code.
- **iOS `AppDelegate.swift`:** the tagged block `with-backup-exclusion-ios` appears once, inside `application(_:didFinishLaunchingWithOptions:)` and before `let delegate = ReactNativeDelegate()`. It builds `.documentDirectory` + `"SQLite"`, creates it if missing, sets `isExcludedFromBackup`, deletes nothing, and its `catch` logs one fixed line with no path and no error (SEC-T026-1 stays fixed).
- **The attribute is really set on the simulators.** This closes the iOS half of SEC-D2-5 / SEC-T026-2: on the iPhone 17 and the iPad Pro 11-inch simulators, `Documents/SQLite` has `com.apple.metadata:com_apple_backup_excludeItem`. A real iPhone or iPad is still on the release list.
- **Same folder as the database:** `src/data/database.ts` calls `openDatabaseSync(DATABASE_NAME, { useNewConnection: true })` with no `directory`, so the file, the WAL and the shared-memory files are in the excluded folder, and the dev inspector never registers the connection. `git grep` finds no `AsyncStorage`, `SecureStore`, `expo-file-system`, `localStorage` or `MMKV` in `src/`: nothing else is stored on the device.
- **iOS `Info.plist` (dev-client prebuild):** `NSAllowsArbitraryLoads` is `false`; `NSAllowsLocalNetworking`, `NSBonjourServices` (`_expo._tcp`) and `NSLocalNetworkUsageDescription` come from `expo-dev-client`; the URL schemes are `planificadorperiodos`, `com.fernando.planificadorperiodos` and `exp+planificador-periodos`. `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption` is not set. No `eas.json` exists. These are the release items of SEC-D2-1 and SEC-D2-2 (SEC-T061-2, SEC-T061-5).
- **Workflow:** `.github/workflows/verify.yml` has `permissions: contents: read`, actions pinned to commit SHAs, and gitleaks and osv-scanner downloaded with SHA-256 checks. Unchanged since the last audit.

### 3. Data layer (`src/data/`)

- **SQL injection: none possible.** Every query is a Drizzle builder with bound values (`select`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, `eq`, `and`, `inArray`, `onConflictDoUpdate`). The only `sql` templates are `${matches.overtimeCount} + 1` (a column reference, no input) and the static `CHECK` constraints in `schema.ts`. The only raw statements are the two fixed `PRAGMA` strings in `database.ts`. No SQL is built by concatenation or interpolation in `src/`.
- **Names stay on the device and out of logs.** No `console`, `NSLog` or log call in `src/` (grep; `eslint.config.js` has `no-console: error` and forbids `fetch`, `XMLHttpRequest`, `WebSocket` and `EventSource`, also through `globalThis`, `window`, `self` and `global`). The only native log is the plugin's fixed line. Errors are dropped on purpose: `database.ts` rethrows, `use-database-start.ts` discards the reason, and the `catch` blocks of the state layer (all without a binding) turn failures into `saveFailed`, `unchanged` or `notFound`. The two `throw new Error` messages in `grid-repository.ts` hold only numeric ids and never reach the screen. No network, `Linking`, `WebBrowser`, share, clipboard or analytics call in `src/`.
- **Migrations:** one migration (`0000_*.sql`: `CREATE TABLE` and `CREATE INDEX` only, no `DROP`, `DELETE` or `ALTER`); `migrations/bundle.ts` is generated by `scripts/bundle-migrations.mjs`, which uses Node built-ins and writes through a temporary file. `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON` and `journal_mode = WAL` run before `migrate`.
- **Error paths (T087):** `openAppDatabase` closes its own client and rethrows when a pragma or the migration fails, and `opened` stays unset; `initializeDatabase` clears its shared promise on failure so the next press of «Reintentar» tries again; the root layout shows only the fixed texts `db.openFailed` and `db.retry`; nothing is deleted or reset. Multi-row writes run in `db.transaction` (`createMatch`, `createPlayerInMatch`, `saveCellChanges`, `removeLastOvertime`), and `saveCellChanges` rejects entries of another match before its first write.
- **Layers:** `expo-sqlite` is imported only by `src/data/database.ts`; `better-sqlite3` and `drizzle-orm/better-sqlite3` only by four files under `tests/`; `src/state/` uses `zustand/vanilla` and `useStore`, with no `devtools`, `persist` or `zustand/middleware`.
- **At rest:** the database is a plain SQLite file in the app sandbox, protected by the operating system (iOS file protection, Android per-app storage and device encryption) and not encrypted by the app. Principle 5 does not ask for more; recorded so nobody assumes otherwise.

### 4. Navigation (`src/app/`)

- **Route parameters:** the seven dynamic routes read `teamId` or `matchId` with `useLocalSearchParams` and parse it with `parseTeamId` or `parseMatchId` (`src/state/route-params.ts`): only the canonical text of a positive safe integer passes; arrays, `01`, `1.0`, `+1`, ` 1`, `1e3` and unsafe numbers give `null`, and the screen shows `NotFound`. No route reads any other parameter and no route reads a query string.
- **Routes carry only numeric ids.** Every `router.push` and `router.replace` builds `/teams/${id}`, `/matches/${id}/...` or the typed `/matches/[matchId]` object from a parsed or database id. Names, categories and notes are in no path. The team name appears only as the native header title.
- **Deep links (`planificadorperiodos://`) trigger nothing destructive.** No route writes on open. The effects in the app are: `loadTeams` (list, `select` only), `useOpenRouteTeam` (F-03: `openTeam`, `select` only), `useOwnSession` (`openMatch`, `select` only) and the new-match draft `reset` (reads the team's players into memory). Every write (`createTeam`, `updateTeam`, `addPlayer`, `createMatch`, `addToCallUp`, `removeFromCallUp`, `createPlayerInMatch`, `saveCellChanges`, `addOvertime`, `removeLastOvertime`, `undo`) is called from a button, a form submit or a cell tap. The F-03 change (opening from a focus effect) did not add a write. The call-up and new-player routes still show `NotFound` unless the open session belongs to the id in the link (`view.matchId !== id`), so a crafted link cannot act on another match. While the database is opening the layout renders `null`, and while it failed it renders only the message, so no route code runs.
- **Typed routes:** `experiments.typedRoutes: true`; the one object-form navigation uses the typed pathname. `tsc` was not run here.
- **No catch-all and no `+not-found`:** a link to a path that matches no route (for example `planificadorperiodos://foo`) falls to Expo Router's default unmatched screen. It performs no action, but its text is not from `es.json` and it was not seen on a device (SEC-T061-4).
- **Android and iOS entry points:** one exported activity with one `VIEW` intent filter for the app scheme (and the dev-client one); the `https` `<data>` entry at the top of the manifest is Expo's `<queries>` block, not an intent filter.

### Findings

| ID         | Severity                               | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Action                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

Whether the Release binary drops them was not checked here (no Gradle or Xcode build).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Remaining part of SEC-D2-1, already in "Before each release": review the merged release manifest and the `Info.plist` of the Release build and MobSF on the APK and IPA; if the keys remain, decide with Fernando how to remove them (a config plugin or excluding `expo-dev-client` from release). |

Development only; not autolinked; pinned by the lock's integrity hash.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | None. If a Dependabot-style update of the lock ever changes its integrity or adds an install script, look at it again.                                                                                                                                                                              |

Closed or confirmed by this audit: SEC-D2-1 (permissions, in the code), SEC-D2-3 (T087), SEC-D2-8, SEC-T026-1, SEC-T026-2 (iOS simulator), SEC-T026-3, SEC-T024-2 and SEC-T024-3 (still hold). The osv-scanner exceptions no longer include the two withdrawn advisories (SEC-SDK58-2 is moot).

### What I could NOT verify

- **No device and no Android emulator.** Not run: the app on screen, a deep link on a device, a real backup or device transfer on Android (E-028), `xattr` on a physical iPhone or iPad, the `NSLog` line when the attribute write fails.
- **No release build.** I read `prebuild` output only (no Gradle or Xcode build, no merged release manifest, no `Info.plist` of a Release build, no APK, AAB or IPA, no MobSF). SEC-T061-1 and SEC-T061-2 stay on the release list for that reason.
- **Package contents.** I read the `package.json`, manifests and sources of the packages in the table in `node_modules`, not every published tarball, and I did not check the app bundle for build-time tools.
- **Tests and tooling:** `tsc`, ESLint, Jest, Stryker and `scripts/verify.sh` were not run, on instruction. The scanners above are the ones `verify.sh` runs, run by hand.
- **GDPR and store declarations** (collects no data, privacy manifest of the iOS build) are release tasks.

### Verdict (Security at the D2 close, T061)

- **Blocking:** none.
- **Important:** two, both for the release and not for D2 (SEC-T061-1, SEC-T061-2), already on the "Before each release" list of `docs/security.md`.
- **Minor:** three (SEC-T061-3, SEC-T061-4, SEC-T061-5). **Informational:** one (SEC-T061-6).
- From a security standpoint T061's condition is met: D2 can close. Principles 3 and 5 hold in the code: no network call, no analytics or third-party SDK, no `console` or log with player data, parameterised queries only, backups excluded on Android (generated manifest and XML) and on iOS (generated code and `xattr` on two simulators), no route writes on open. Files changed by this audit: this section only.

## Mutation testing at the D2 close (T061) (2026-11-21)

Branch `docs/t061-d2-close`, from `main` at `b8a57c5`. No code changed. This section closes finding I2 of the D2 verification (the 19 survivors outside `undo.ts` were not listed one by one) and records the mutation times T061 asks for.

### Local runs (10-core Mac, 4 test runners and 5 checkers)

| Run                                                      | Command                                                                                    | Time                   | Result                                                                              |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Full (every mutant, no incremental file, fresh checkout) | `npx stryker run --incremental --incrementalFile reports/stryker-incremental.json --force` | **353 s (5 min 53 s)** | 804 mutants: 470 killed, 20 timed out, 27 survived, 0 without coverage; **94.78 %** |
| Incremental, right after the full run                    | the same command without `--force`                                                         | **28 s**               | Same result; 789 of the 804 results came from the file and 15 were run again        |

`ios/` and `android/` did not exist in this worktree (fresh checkout), so Stryker did not fail copying `ios/Pods`. The score (94.78 %, threshold 80 %) and the survivor count (27) are the same as in the verification of 2026-11-13; the D1 run recorded 93.89 % with the same 27 survivors.

### CI runs (GitHub Actions, workflow `verify`, from `gh run list`)

- **Runs on a pull request:** 3 to 5 minutes. They print "Mutation testing (skipped: no domain changes)", because none of the PRs of 2026-11-20 and 2026-11-21 touches `src/domain/`.
- **Runs on `main` after each merge** (`STRYKER_INCREMENTAL=1` and `STRYKER_FORCE=1` in the workflow): 18 to 30 minutes in total. In run 44943343890 (`main` at `b8a57c5`) the mutation step went from 19:56:52 to 20:12:17, about **15 min 25 s**, 804 mutants, **94.78 %**, with the cache restored ("Cache restored successfully", 183 KB) and then rebuilt by `--force`. So on `main` the cache is restored but never used to skip mutants; on a PR that changes the domain it would be used.
- The CI runs right after T024, T080, T028 and T029 on the D2 branch are not in this list (only the latest 25 runs were read); the numbers above are the ones measured on 2026-11-21.

### The 19 survivors outside `undo.ts` (27 in total, the 8 of `undo.ts` are classified above)

The Stryker clear-text output lists them; every one was read against the code. None is a real gap of a D2 behaviour.

**`bounded-flow.ts` (9), all equivalent.** The flow network of "rule impossible" (RF-067) only reads cells that were written first.

| Line         | Mutation                                                  | Class      | Why                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 62:27, 62:72 | `via` is `new Array()` / filled with `+1` instead of `-1` | Equivalent | `via` is read only for nodes the search reached, and those were written first (`via[arc.to] = index`). The initial value is never read.                                                          |
| 63:18        | `seen` is `new Array()`                                   | Equivalent | An unset `seen[x]` is `undefined`, which is falsy like `false`.                                                                                                                                  |
| 64:20        | `seen[source] = false`                                    | Equivalent | The source can only be reached again through an arc that comes back to it; its arcs were all explored at the first step and capacities do not change during one search, so nothing new is found. |
| 66:24        | `head <= queue.length`                                    | Equivalent | The extra step reads `queue[length]` = `undefined`; `outgoing[undefined] ?? []` is empty.                                                                                                        |
| 91:18        | `excess` is `new Array()`                                 | Equivalent | `excess[x] ?? 0` already covers the unset nodes, and a node with excess 0 adds no arc.                                                                                                           |
| 105:9        | `value >= 0`                                              | Equivalent | An arc of capacity 0 from the super-source is never used (`capacity <= 0` is skipped) and adds 0 to `required`.                                                                                  |
| 108:16 (two) | `else if (true)` / `value <= 0`                           | Equivalent | Here `value` is already `<= 0`; for `0` the arc to the super-sink has capacity 0, never used.                                                                                                    |

**`marking.ts` (5), all defence only.** They are the second guards of functions whose caller already refuses a cell or column outside the grid (`canOpenCellMenu` in `cell-menu.ts:36`, `cellExists` in `tapCell`). They fire only with a cell or a column that is not in the grid, which no real action builds (RF-086, property test "no marks outside the grid").

| Line        | Mutation                                                       | Class                                                                                                        |
| ----------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 68:7        | `markSub`: `if (!cellExists(...)) return grid` → `if (false)`  | Defence only (the only caller, `chooseCellMenuOption`, has returned `unchanged` for a cell outside the grid) |
| 103:7       | `removeMark`: same guard                                       | Defence only (same caller; and removing an absent mark returns an equal grid)                                |
| 79:21 (two) | `replaceableStarters`: `columnKey(c) === key` → `true` / `!==` | Defence only (only matters for a column not in the grid, which has no marks, so the list is `[]` anyway)     |
| 81:7        | `if (!inGrid) return []` → `if (false)`                        | Defence only (same reason)                                                                                   |

**`rule-feasibility.ts` (2), equivalent.** Line 12:7, `players < MAX_STARTERS_PER_PERIOD` → `false` or `players <= ...`: the early `true` is a shortcut. With fewer than 5 players the flow cannot give 5 X to a window period, and with exactly 5 players every one would have to play every window period, which breaks the minimum of rests (`minRested` is 1 or 2). So the answer is `true` either way.

**`column.ts` (1), defence only.** Line 48:5, `column.number >= 1` → `true` in `isRuleWindowColumn`: a period with number 0 or less is never built (`periods()` starts at 1). A one-line test with `{ kind: "period", number: 0 }` would kill it; it was not added because it would change `src/domain/` tests in a docs-only close (see "Open decision" below).

**`overtime.ts` (1), equivalent.** Line 11:7, `if (grid.overtimeCount === 0) return []` → `if (false)`: the keys of an overtime number 0 (`OT0`) never hold a mark, so `lastOvertimeHasMarks` is `false` either way, and `lastOvertimeKeys` has no other caller.

**`player-order.ts` (1), equivalent.** Line 21:12, `valueA < valueB` → `<=`: that line only runs when `valueA !== valueB`, so `<=` and `<` are the same.

**Total:** 19 survivors, 13 equivalent (`bounded-flow.ts` 9, `rule-feasibility.ts` 2, `overtime.ts` 1, `player-order.ts` 1) and 6 defence only (`marking.ts` 5, `column.ts` 1). No D2 domain file has a survivor (`squad-validation.ts`, `match-input.ts`, `match-date.ts`: 0). I2 is closed.

### Open decision

- Add the one-line test for `column.ts:48` (and tests passing a cell outside the grid to `markSub` and `removeMark`) to reach 100 % on these files? It would raise the score by about 0.4 points and change nothing in the app. Recommended: no; the survivors are documented and the threshold is met with margin.
